import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react"; import { LogOut, Settings, Save, Check, Play } from "lucide-react"; import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-app.js"; import { getFirestore, collection, addDoc, serverTimestamp, query, where, orderBy, limit, getDocs, doc, setDoc, getDoc, onSnapshot, } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js"; const firebaseConfig = { apiKey: "AIzaSyC7lsAItaW_b8y3GNyCHN51UwZpwzjOnak", authDomain: "platforma-antrenamente.firebaseapp.com", projectId: "platforma-antrenamente", storageBucket: "platforma-antrenamente.firebasestorage.app", messagingSenderId: "188929299201", appId: "1:188929299201:web:2513b2addaec40b3ddc100", }; const firebaseApp = initializeApp(firebaseConfig); const db = getFirestore(firebaseApp); const ISTORIC_COLLECTION = "istoric_greutati"; const ACTIVITY_COLLECTION = "activitate"; const GREUTATI_ACTIVE_COLLECTION = "greutati_active"; const FEEDBACK_COLLECTION = "feedback_antrenament"; // Salveaza feedback-ul unei zile de antrenament (un doc per client+luna+zi, suprascrie) async function saveFeedback({ cod_client, luna, ziIndex, ziLabel, rating }) { const docId = `${cod_client.toUpperCase()}_${luna}_zi${ziIndex}`; await setDoc(doc(db, FEEDBACK_COLLECTION, docId), { cod_client: cod_client.toUpperCase(), luna: String(luna), ziIndex: Number(ziIndex), ziLabel: ziLabel || "", rating, // "usor" | "perfect" | "greu" data: serverTimestamp(), }); } // Incarca tot feedback-ul unui client (toate lunile) async function loadFeedback(codClient) { try { const snap = await getDocs(query(collection(db, FEEDBACK_COLLECTION), where("cod_client", "==", codClient.toUpperCase()))); return snap.docs.map((d) => d.data()); } catch { return []; } } // Salveaza starea curenta a greutatilor unui client (snapshot complet) async function saveActiveWeights(codClient, luna, weights) { const docId = `${codClient.toUpperCase()}_${luna}`; await setDoc(doc(db, GREUTATI_ACTIVE_COLLECTION, docId), { cod_client: codClient.toUpperCase(), luna: String(luna), weights, updatedAt: serverTimestamp(), }); } // Incarca starea curenta a greutatilor unui client async function loadActiveWeights(codClient, luna) { const docId = `${codClient.toUpperCase()}_${luna}`; const snap = await getDoc(doc(db, GREUTATI_ACTIVE_COLLECTION, docId)); if (snap.exists()) { return snap.data().weights || {}; } return null; // null = nu exista in Firebase } async function saveWeightToHistory({ cod_client, luna, ziua, exercitiu, saptamana, serie, greutate }) { await addDoc(collection(db, ISTORIC_COLLECTION), { cod_client, luna: String(luna), ziua: ziua || "", exercitiu, saptamana, serie: Number(serie), greutate: String(greutate), data: serverTimestamp(), }); } async function loadHistoryFromFirestore(codClient) { const q = query( collection(db, ISTORIC_COLLECTION), where("cod_client", "==", codClient.toUpperCase()) ); const snap = await getDocs(q); const rows = snap.docs.map((d) => { const v = d.data(); const ts = v.data && typeof v.data.toDate === "function" ? v.data.toDate() : null; return { cod_client: v.cod_client || "", luna: v.luna || "", ziua: v.ziua || "", exercitiu: v.exercitiu || "", saptamana: v.saptamana || "", serie: v.serie || "", greutate: v.greutate || "", data: ts ? ts.toISOString() : "", _sortTime: ts ? ts.getTime() : 0, }; }); rows.sort((a, b) => a._sortTime - b._sortTime); return rows; } // Inregistreaza un eveniment de activitate (login) — best-effort, nu blocheaza UI-ul clientului async function logActivity({ cod_client, tip, detaliu, luna }) { try { await addDoc(collection(db, ACTIVITY_COLLECTION), { cod_client, tip, detaliu: detaliu || "", luna: luna ? String(luna) : "", data: serverTimestamp(), }); } catch (e) { // ignore — activitatea e doar informativa, nu trebuie sa strice experienta clientului } } // Aduce statistici per client: sedinte (login-uri unice pe zile), prima si ultima data, din luna curenta async function loadClientStats(codClient, luna) { const [loginsSnap, editsSnap] = await Promise.all([ getDocs(query(collection(db, ACTIVITY_COLLECTION), where("cod_client", "==", codClient), where("luna", "==", String(luna)))), getDocs(query(collection(db, ACTIVITY_COLLECTION), where("cod_client", "==", codClient))), ]); // Toate login-urile pentru acest client (orice luna) const allLogins = editsSnap.docs.map((d) => { const v = d.data(); const ts = v.data && typeof v.data.toDate === "function" ? v.data.toDate() : null; return { luna: v.luna || "", time: ts }; }).filter((x) => x.time); // Login-urile din luna curenta const lunaLogins = loginsSnap.docs.map((d) => { const v = d.data(); const ts = v.data && typeof v.data.toDate === "function" ? v.data.toDate() : null; return ts; }).filter(Boolean); // Numar zile unice cu sedinta in luna curenta const zileCuSedinta = new Set(lunaLogins.map((ts) => ts.toISOString().slice(0, 10))); const sedinte = zileCuSedinta.size; const primaSedinta = lunaLogins.length > 0 ? new Date(Math.min(...lunaLogins.map((t) => t.getTime()))) : null; const ultimaActivitate = allLogins.length > 0 ? new Date(Math.max(...allLogins.map((x) => x.time.getTime()))) : null; return { sedinte, primaSedinta, ultimaActivitate }; } // Numara total zile-antrenament unice din istoricul incarcat (fara request nou) function computeStreak(rows) { const zile = new Set(); rows.forEach((r) => { if (r.data) zile.add(String(r.data).slice(0, 10)); }); return zile.size; } // Aduce toate login-urile pentru toti clientii (pentru dashboard) async function loadAllClientsActivity() { const snap = await getDocs(query(collection(db, ACTIVITY_COLLECTION), orderBy("data", "desc"), limit(500))); return snap.docs.map((d) => { const v = d.data(); const ts = v.data && typeof v.data.toDate === "function" ? v.data.toDate() : null; return { cod_client: v.cod_client || "", luna: v.luna || "", time: ts }; }).filter((x) => x.time); } // Aduce ultimele intrari combinate: login-uri + editari de greutati, sortate descrescator dupa timp async function loadRecentActivity(maxItems = 20) { const [loginsSnap, editsSnap] = await Promise.all([ getDocs(query(collection(db, ACTIVITY_COLLECTION), orderBy("data", "desc"), limit(maxItems))), getDocs(query(collection(db, ISTORIC_COLLECTION), orderBy("data", "desc"), limit(maxItems))), ]); const logins = loginsSnap.docs.map((d) => { const v = d.data(); const ts = v.data && typeof v.data.toDate === "function" ? v.data.toDate() : null; return { cod_client: v.cod_client || "", tip: "login", detaliu: "", time: ts }; }); const edits = editsSnap.docs.map((d) => { const v = d.data(); const ts = v.data && typeof v.data.toDate === "function" ? v.data.toDate() : null; return { cod_client: v.cod_client || "", tip: "editare", detaliu: v.exercitiu || "", time: ts }; }); return [...logins, ...edits] .filter((x) => x.time) .sort((a, b) => b.time.getTime() - a.time.getTime()) .slice(0, maxItems); } function formatRelativeTime(date) { if (!date) return ""; const diffSec = Math.floor((Date.now() - date.getTime()) / 1000); if (diffSec < 60) return "chiar acum"; const diffMin = Math.floor(diffSec / 60); if (diffMin < 60) return `acum ${diffMin} ${diffMin === 1 ? "minut" : "minute"}`; const diffH = Math.floor(diffMin / 60); if (diffH < 24) return `acum ${diffH} ${diffH === 1 ? "oră" : "ore"}`; const diffD = Math.floor(diffH / 24); return `acum ${diffD} ${diffD === 1 ? "zi" : "zile"}`; } // ─── Auto-login helpers (localStorage) ──────────────────────────────────── const AUTO_LOGIN_KEY = "razone_autologin_v1"; // salvează codul clientului, nu al adminului function getAutoLoginCode() { try { return localStorage.getItem(AUTO_LOGIN_KEY) || null; } catch { return null; } } function saveAutoLoginCode(code) { try { localStorage.setItem(AUTO_LOGIN_KEY, code.toUpperCase()); } catch {} } function clearAutoLoginCode() { try { localStorage.removeItem(AUTO_LOGIN_KEY); } catch {} } // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── const RED = "#22D3AA"; // Aptra brand: Signal green (accent principal) const SIGNAL = "#22D3AA"; const SIGNAL_DARK = "#062d24"; const GLOBAL_GLOSSARY = { "Tempo": "Fiecare repetare are 3 faze: faza negativă (excentrică) — coborâre lentă, în 3 secunde; faza izometrică — menții poziția 1 secundă; faza pozitivă (concentrică) — ridicare explozivă.", "Rep": "Alege o greutate cu care poți duce seria până pe la numărul de repetări scris (de exemplu, la „12 rep”, greutatea trebuie să te ducă până pe la 12 repetări). ); } // ─── Grafic progres per exercitiu (SVG pur, fara librarii) ────────────────── // Primeste randuri din istoric_greutati si deseneaza greutatea maxima / saptamana. function buildProgressSeries(rows) { // Grupeaza dupa exercitiu -> luna -> saptamana, retine greutatea maxima const byEx = {}; rows.forEach((r) => { const g = parseFloat(String(r.greutate).replace(",", ".")); if (!r.exercitiu || isNaN(g)) return; const ex = r.exercitiu; const key = `${r.luna}|${r.saptamana}`; if (!byEx[ex]) byEx[ex] = {}; if (byEx[ex][key] == null || g > byEx[ex][key].max) { byEx[ex][key] = { max: g, luna: r.luna, saptamana: r.saptamana, t: r._sortTime || 0 }; } else if (g > byEx[ex][key].max) { byEx[ex][key].max = g; } }); const result = {}; Object.keys(byEx).forEach((ex) => { const pts = Object.values(byEx[ex]).sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t); if (pts.length >= 1) result[ex] = pts; }); return result; } // ─── Progresie automata: sugereaza greutatea urmatoare per exercitiu ──────── // Detecteaza daca e exercitiu mare (compus) sau izolare, pentru incrementul corect. function isCompoundLift(name) { const n = String(name).toLowerCase(); const compound = ["bench", "press", "deadlift", "squat", "row", "pull", "hip thrust", "lunge", "leg press", "dip", "chin"]; const isolation = ["curl", "extension", "fly", "flies", "lateral", "raise", "kickback", "pushdown", "pec deck", "calf", "shrug", "rear delt", "face pull"]; // izolarile au prioritate daca numele contine un termen de izolare if (isolation.some((k) => n.includes(k))) return false; if (compound.some((k) => n.includes(k))) return true; return false; // default: tratam ca izolare (increment mic, mai sigur) } // Din istoric -> map: numele exercitiului -> { last, suggested } // feedbackByZi: opcional { ziLabel(lowercase): "usor"|"perfect"|"greu" } pentru ajustare inteligenta function computeSuggestions(rows, feedbackByZi) { const byEx = {}; rows.forEach((r) => { const g = parseFloat(String(r.greutate).replace(",", ".")); if (!r.exercitiu || isNaN(g)) return; const t = r._sortTime || 0; const ex = r.exercitiu; if (!byEx[ex] || t > byEx[ex].t || (t === byEx[ex].t && g > byEx[ex].last)) { if (!byEx[ex] || t > byEx[ex].t) byEx[ex] = { t, last: g, ziua: r.ziua || "" }; else if (g > byEx[ex].last) byEx[ex].last = g; } }); const out = {}; Object.keys(byEx).forEach((ex) => { const last = byEx[ex].last; let inc = isCompoundLift(ex) ? 2.5 : 1.25; // Ajustare pe baza feedback-ului ultimei sesiuni din ziua exercitiului if (feedbackByZi && byEx[ex].ziua) { const fb = feedbackByZi[String(byEx[ex].ziua).toLowerCase()]; if (fb === "usor") inc = inc * 2; // a fost usor -> creste mai mult else if (fb === "greu") inc = 0; // a fost greu -> repeta aceeasi greutate } const suggested = Math.round((last + inc) * 4) / 4; out[ex] = { last, suggested }; }); return out; } // Verifica daca o greutate noua e record personal la un exercitiu (mai mare ca tot istoricul de dinainte) function isPersonalRecord(rows, exerciseName, newWeight) { const g = parseFloat(String(newWeight).replace(",", ".")); if (isNaN(g) || g <= 0 || !exerciseName) return false; let maxBefore = 0; rows.forEach((r) => { if (r.exercitiu !== exerciseName) return; const w = parseFloat(String(r.greutate).replace(",", ".")); if (!isNaN(w) && w > maxBefore) maxBefore = w; }); return maxBefore > 0 && g > maxBefore; } function ProgressChart({ points, dark }) { // points: [{max, luna, saptamana}] const stroke = dark ? "#ef4444" : RED; const grid = dark ? "#262626" : "#e2e8f0"; const txt = dark ? "#9ca3af" : "#64748b"; const dot = dark ? "#fca5a5" : RED; if (!points || points.length === 0) { return
Nicio greutate înregistrată.
; } const W = 320, H = 160, padL = 34, padR = 12, padT = 14, padB = 26; const vals = points.map((p) => p.max); let min = Math.min(...vals), max = Math.max(...vals); if (min === max) { min = min - 2; max = max + 2; } const range = max - min || 1; const innerW = W - padL - padR, innerH = H - padT - padB; const xFor = (i) => padL + (points.length === 1 ? innerW / 2 : (i / (points.length - 1)) * innerW); const yFor = (v) => padT + innerH - ((v - min) / range) * innerH; const linePath = points.map((p, i) => `${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${xFor(i).toFixed(1)},${yFor(p.max).toFixed(1)}`).join(" "); const areaPath = `${linePath} L${xFor(points.length - 1).toFixed(1)},${(padT + innerH).toFixed(1)} L${xFor(0).toFixed(1)},${(padT + innerH).toFixed(1)} Z`; const first = points[0].max, last = points[points.length - 1].max; const diff = last - first; const trendColor = diff > 0 ? (dark ? "#4ade80" : "#16a34a") : diff < 0 ? "#ef4444" : txt; const trendTxt = diff > 0 ? `↑ +${diff.toFixed(1)} kg` : diff < 0 ? `↓ ${diff.toFixed(1)} kg` : "→ constant"; return (
{points.length} măsurători {trendTxt}
); } // Modal reutilizabil cu selector de exercitiu + grafic // Genereaza un PDF de PROGRES (doar statistici + evolutie, NU programul). // Protejeaza IP-ul antrenorului: fara exercitii-serii-tehnici, doar evolutia clientului. async function generateProgressPDF(clientName, rows) { // Incarca librariile o singura data if (!window.html2canvas) { await new Promise((res, rej) => { const s = document.createElement("script"); s.src = "https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/html2canvas/1.4.1/html2canvas.min.js"; s.onload = res; s.onerror = rej; document.head.appendChild(s); }); } if (!window.jspdf) { await new Promise((res, rej) => { const s = document.createElement("script"); s.src = "https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/jspdf/2.5.1/jspdf.umd.min.js"; s.onload = res; s.onerror = rej; document.head.appendChild(s); }); } const series = buildProgressSeries(rows); const exNames = Object.keys(series).sort(); // Statistici agregate let totalRecorduri = 0; const zile = new Set(); rows.forEach((r) => { const g = parseFloat(String(r.greutate).replace(",", ".")); if (!isNaN(g) && g > 0 && r.data) zile.add(String(r.data).slice(0, 10)); }); const evolutii = exNames.map((ex) => { const pts = series[ex]; const start = pts[0]?.max || 0; const now = pts[pts.length - 1]?.max || 0; const diff = now - start; if (diff > 0) totalRecorduri++; return { ex, start, now, diff }; }).filter(e => e.now > 0).sort((a, b) => b.diff - a.diff); const azi = new Date().toLocaleDateString("ro-RO", { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" }); // Construieste HTML-ul raportului const W = 620; const c = document.createElement("div"); c.style.cssText = `position:fixed;top:-9999px;left:-9999px;background:#0f1115;padding:32px;width:${W}px;box-sizing:border-box;font-family:'Space Grotesk',system-ui,sans-serif;`; const rowsHtml = evolutii.map((e) => { const semn = e.diff > 0 ? "+" : ""; const culoare = e.diff > 0 ? "#22D3AA" : e.diff < 0 ? "#ef4444" : "#6b7280"; return ` ${e.ex} ${e.start} kg ${e.now} kg ${semn}${e.diff.toFixed(1)} kg `; }).join(""); c.innerHTML = `

`; document.body.appendChild(c); try { const canvas = await window.html2canvas(c, { backgroundColor: "#0f1115", scale: 2.5, useCORS: true, logging: false }); const imgData = canvas.toDataURL("image/png"); const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf; const pdf = new jsPDF({ orientation: "portrait", unit: "px", format: "a4" }); const pw = pdf.internal.pageSize.getWidth(); const ph = (canvas.height * pw) / canvas.width; pdf.addImage(imgData, "PNG", 0, 0, pw, Math.min(ph, pdf.internal.pageSize.getHeight())); pdf.save(`RAZONE_Progres_${clientName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, "_")}.pdf`); } finally { document.body.removeChild(c); } } function ProgressModal({ rows, dark, onClose, title = "Progresul meu", clientName = "Client" }) { const series = React.useMemo(() => buildProgressSeries(rows), [rows]); const [pdfBusy, setPdfBusy] = useState(false); const exNames = Object.keys(series).sort(); const [selected, setSelected] = useState(exNames[0] || null); const cur = selected && series[selected] ? series[selected] : []; const bg = dark ? { ...b, exercises: b.exercises.filter((_, j) => j !== ei) } : b)); }; // Schimba nr serii per saptamana pt un exercitiu const updateSeriesCount = (bi, ei, wi, count) => { setBlocks(prev => prev.map((b, i) => { if (i !== bi) return b; return { ...b, exercises: b.exercises.map((ex, j) => { if (j !== ei) return ex; const newWeeks = ex.weeks.map((wk, wii) => { if (wii !== wi) return wk; const cur = wk.cells.length; const target = Number(count); let cells = [...wk.cells]; while (cells.length < target) cells.push({ type: "normal", label: "10" }); cells = cells.slice(0, target); return { cells }; }); return { ...ex, weeks: newWeeks }; }), }; })); }; // Schimba label repetari pt o celula const updateCellLabel = (bi, ei, wi, ci, val) => { setBlocks(prev => prev.map((b, i) => { if (i !== bi) return b; return { ...b, exercises: b.exercises.map((ex, j) => { if (j !== ei) return ex; const newWeeks = ex.weeks.map((wk, wii) => { if (wii !== wi) return wk; const cells = wk.cells.map((c, cii) => cii === ci ?
